Create nodes\n    nodes = [TreeNode(i) for i in range(1, len(indexes) + 1)]\n    \n    # Build tree\n    for i in range(len(indexes)):\n        left, right = indexes[i]\n        if left != -1:\n            nodes[i+1].left = nodes[left]\n            nodes[i+1].right = nodes[right]\n        else:\n            nodes[i+1].left = None\n            nodes[i+1].right = nodes[right]\n    \n    # Find depths\n    depths = [-1] * (len(indexes) + 1)\n    queue = deque([1])\n    depths[1] = 1\n    \n    while queue:\n        node = queue.popleft()\n        left_child = nodes[node].left\n        right_child = nodes[node].right\n        \n        if left_child:\n            depths[left_child.val] = depths[node] + 1\n            queue.append(left_child)\n        \n        if right_child:\n            depths[right_child.val] = depths[node] + 1\n            queue.append(right_child)\n    \n    # Find depths\n    depths = [-1] * (len(indexes) + 1)\n    queue = deque([1])\n    depths[1] = 1\n    \n    while queue:\n        node = queue.popleft()\n        left_child = nodes[node].left\n        right_child = nodes[node].right\n        \n        if left_child:\n            depths[left_child.val] = depths[node] + 1\n            queue.append(left_child)\n        \n        if right_child:\n            depths[right_child.val] = depths[node] + 1\n            queue.append(right_child)\n    \n    # In-order traversal function\n    def inorder(node):\n        if not node:\n            return []\n        return inorder(node.left) + [node.val] + inorder(node.right)\n    \n    # Process queries\n    result = []\n    for k in queries:\n        # Make a copy of the tree structure\n        new_nodes = [TreeNode(nodes[i].val) for i in range(len(indexes) + 1)]\n        \n        # Copy connections\n        for i in range(len(indexes)):\n            left, right = indexes[i]\n            if left != -1:\n                new_nodes[i+1].left = new_nodes[left]\n                new_nodes[i+1].right = new_nodes[right]\n            else:\n                new_nodes[i+1].left = None\n                new_nodes[i+1].right = new_nodes[right]\n        \n        # Swap subtrees at depths that are multiples of k\n        for i in range(1, len(indexes) + 1):\n            if depths[i] % k == 0:\n                # Get current children\n                left = new_nodes[i].left\n                right = new_nodes[i].right\n                # Swap\n                new_nodes[i].left = right\n                new_nodes[i].right = left\n        \n        # Get inorder traversal\n        traversal = inorder(new_nodes[1])\n        result.append(traversal)\n    \n    return result\n\n# Read input and solve\nn = int(input())\nindexes = []\nfor _ in range(n):\n    line = input().split()\n    a, b = int(line[0]), int(line[1])\n    indexes.append((a, b))\n\nq = int(input())\nqueries = []\nfor _ in range(q):\n    queries.append(int(input()))\n\n# Get result\nresult = swapNodes(indexes, queries)\n\n# Print results\nfor row in result:\n    print(' '.join(map(str, row)))"}}

Parse the input\n    for i in range(len(indexes)):\n        left, right = indexes[i]\n        if left != -1:\n            children[i+1].append(left)\n        if right != -1:\n            children[i+1].append(right)\n    \n    # Calculate depths using BFS\n    depths = {1: 1}\n    queue = deque([1])\n    \n    while queue:\n        node = queue.popleft()\n        \n        for child in children[node]:\n            depths[child] = depths[node] + 1\n            queue.append(child)\n    \n    # Function to perform in-order traversal\n    def inorder(node):\n        if node == 0:  # No node\n            return []\n        \n        # Find left and right children\n        if len(children[node]) >= 2:\n            left = children[node][0]\n            right = children[node][1]\n        elif len(children[node]) == 1:\n            left = None\n            right = children[node][0]\n        else:\n            left = None\n            right = None\n        \n        result = []\n        # If left child exists, traverse it\n        if left is not None:\n            result.extend(inorder(left))\n        # Add current node\n        result.append(node)\n        # If right child exists, traverse it\n        if right is not None:\n            result.extend(inorder(right))\n        return result\n    \n    # Process queries\n    result = []\n    for k in queries:\n        # Get depths that are multiples of k\n        target_depths = set()\n        max_depth = max(depths.values()) if depths else 0\n        for d in range(k, max_depth + 1, k):\n            target_depths.add(d)\n        \n        # Swap left and right children for each node\n            for node in nodes_at_depth:\n                if len(new_children[node]) >= 2:\n                    # Swap first and second children\n                    new_children[node][0], new_children[node][1] = new_children[node][1], new_children[node][0]\n        \n        #
